# Syncmoney 1.4.0 AI Execution Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** 先讀00-AGENT-ENTRY，再只讀當前task、Required references與必要source，不預載全套文件。使用者已授權依本計畫自主技術決策、實作、驗證、更新紀錄及分層commit；release/PR/merge仍依計畫release gates推進。

**Goal:** 執行 [04 主設計](04-architecture-and-implementation-plan.md)；順序是可重現 correctness、明確 contracts、模組提取、runtime驗證與發布。

**Architecture:** 純Java core透過 ports工作；Bukkit組裝所有 adapters為一個JAR。保留 legacy FQCN、資源根、root version/task/artifact paths。API小、protocol v1不改wire。

**Tech Stack:** Java25/Gradle9.8.0/JUnit5/Mockito；既有Jedis/JDBC/Undertow/Vue；benchmark新增JMH/HdrHistogram。

**Spec:** `04-architecture-and-implementation-plan.md`、三份 source audits、`06-validation-benchmark-migration.md`。

## Global Constraints

- 1.4.0 toolchain／`options.release`／最低runtime／API consumer固定Java25，Gradle wrapper固定9.8.0；所有1.4 Java modules和acceptance/benchmark一致，無preview features。1.3.2歷史fixture/artifact仍保持原版本建置。
- 繼承04 Global Constraints；`server-name`、config12、schema身份、舊 key/channel、SQL `player_uuid` 不改。
- Scope僅1.4foundation，不加入ledger/currency/coordinator/Aerontransport。
- 禁止尚未確認receipt的memory optimistic revision進sharedSQL；event/codecs不可把version二次GET與balance拼湊。
- 每task先讀implementation/callers/tests；既有問題先reproducer再小修，移動檔案時不混無關semantic changes。
- Main/API/legacyreturnFQCN保持；API不relocate、不在consumer shade；testkit不進runtime。
- 以下新class/method為候選；未出現於repo不代表遺失實作。先以證據核准必要type，不把檔案清單當必建framework。
- 實作時commit依當時使用者授權；本計畫不要求現在建立任何commit。未授權publish不能自行Central/GitHub release。

## Review Focus

冷帳戶不可假0覆蓋；拒絕第二腿不可丟funds；sentunknown不可盲retry；logout/eviction不可丟accepted；舊SQLwriter/CMIclock不得偽稱monotonic。分別由T01/T04、T02/T07、T06/T08、T03/T15、T05/T13/T21負責。

## 工作目錄与未来文件图

命令一律從repository root執行，唯frontend命令使用 `pnpm --dir syncmoney-web`。資料夾搬移前src路徑如現況；T12/T14後使用下表destination，不能同FQCN兩份source同時編譯。

| 類型 | future path / owner |
|---|---|
| approved read API signatures（ADR-03） | `syncmoney-api/src/main/java/noietime/syncmoney/api/{SyncmoneyApi,EconomyService,BalanceSnapshot}.java` |
| purecore state | `syncmoney-core/src/main/java/noietime/syncmoney/core/{AccountSnapshot,AccountStateStore,MoneyRules,MutationOutcome,MutationCommand,MutationEnvelope,TransferEnvelope,DefaultEconomyEngine}.java` |
| core ports | `syncmoney-core/src/main/java/noietime/syncmoney/core/port/{WriteAdmission,AdmissionToken,AccountLoader,PersistencePort,ReplicationPort,TransactionPolicy,TransactionObserver,RuntimeCounters}.java` |
| protocol | `syncmoney-protocol/src/main/java/noietime/syncmoney/protocol/{RedisKeys,RedisChannels,LegacyBalanceMessage,LegacyCmiMessage,LegacyProtocolCodec,ProtocolCapabilities}.java`、`src/main/resources/lua/` |
| storage | `syncmoney-storage/src/main/java/noietime/syncmoney/storage/`保持既有storage FQCN；candidate（ADR-07必要性核准後） `persistence/{PersistedAccount,JdbcSnapshotRepository,LocalSnapshotRepository,RecoverySpool}.java`；`LocalEconomyHandler`舊FQCNwrapper留bukkit |
| sync | `syncmoney-sync/src/main/java/noietime/syncmoney/replication/{RedisReplicationAdapter,RequestExecutionState}.java`；receipt/ConfirmedAccount是core port DTO，既有`sync`有Bukkit依賴者wrapper留bukkit |
| platform | `syncmoney-bukkit/src/main/java/noietime/syncmoney/`保留main/context/legacy class；new `adapter/{AsyncAccountLoader,BukkitApiProvider,LocalPersistenceWorker,BukkitTransactionHooks}.java` |
| tests/fixtures | `syncmoney-testkit/src/testFixtures/java/noietime/syncmoney/testkit/`、`src/testFixtures/resources/compat/1.3.2/` |
| benchmark | `syncmoney-benchmarks/src/jmh/java/noietime/syncmoney/benchmark/`、`src/loadTest/java/.../OpenLoopLoadRunner.java`、`profiles/*.json` |
| resources | root `src/main/resources`仍canonical descriptors/YAML/webassets；Lua在T10有一份canonical，processResources聚合至原JAR位置 |
| automation | T18選型ADR與既有release/CI workflow；新增tool-specific檔案依ADR決定，custom script不是預定產物 |

上表是責任與候選路徑地圖，不是強制建立所有new types的manifest；API surface依已resolved ADR-03，其餘新engine/ports按ADR-07最小必要性選定。不能機械搬整個package：`StorageManager`仍Bukkit lifecycle，`CMIPubsubHandler`仍platform entityadapter；純protocol不持有Jedis，core不持有Plugin。

## Module scaffold ownership／future Gradle task routing

Minimal scaffold只建settings include、必要build/dependency/test source與task，root既有production sources/assembly仍保留；不copy同FQCN到兩個active source sets、不提前搬整個repository。當前不存在的Gradle命令是future contract，執行前由下列owner確認已建立；否則先用root測試驗行為，不skip當PASS。

| Task owner | Minimal scaffold when required | 完整搬移／禁止提前工作 |
|---|---|---|
| T00/T01/T02 | root characterization/fixture paths；T00 fixture可先放未include目錄 | 不為manifest/triage建立全module DAG；T02新outcome可暫root |
| T03 | syncmoney-core test-only minimal scaffold；若選定port/DTO需要API先stub-free JDK測試 | 不執行T12 core extraction；core->api最終edge待T11/T12 |
| T05 | syncmoney-storage scaffold與必要integrationTest owner | 不搬全部StorageManager／platform lifecycle |
| T06 | syncmoney-sync scaffold、必要integrationTest；早期Lua仍用既有canonical資源 | 不提前T10 protocol搬移、T14 assembly |
| T10 | syncmoney-protocol完整protocol提取／測試 | 消除早期Lua引用重複，保留原JAR路徑 |
| T11 | syncmoney-api、check/test/javadoc/sourcesJar與provider candidate | ADR-03已核准read-only scope；不在此task發布artifact |
| T12 | complete core extraction、core verifyArchitecture | 只提取已選interface與state；沒有caller不建type |
| T14 | syncmoney-bukkit、final DAG/assembly、root aliases、verifyArchitecture/verifyPackaging | 保證單JAR與reflection/resource identity |
| T17 | syncmoney-testkit java-test-fixtures與共享helper | 前置task可用local fixtures，不等待generic framework |
| T19 | syncmoney-benchmarks、benchmarkJar/runBaselineComparison | benchmark非runtime artifact |
| Existing | syncmoney-papi-expansion、syncmoney-web | 不新增部署步驟；frontend不是Java scaffold |

任何early scaffold改動記05 Interfaces交接：exact path/signature、temporary dependency、Gradle task owner、T12/T14撤除/整合條件與duplicate-class check。T05/T06的integrationTest若backend不可得標BLOCKED，不用compile success替代。已存在scaffold則重用，不重建。

## Internal interface candidates：不是整套engine rewrite前置

04§5/10/11/21約束必須遵守，但前版長篇AccountEntry/PendingOperation/AdmissionToken/TransferEnvelope/RecoverySpool signatures不是已freeze API。它們僅internal candidate；T01再現後依ADR-07逐項採最小修復，在既有writer/queue/consumer可解決時不另造全狀態engine。

| task | 必須證明的contract | candidate／不強制 |
|---|---|---|
| T03 | 接受前有界容量、未接受不改money、commit完整、retry不早釋capacity | reservation token可以採；保留原queue亦可，須證明同fault suite |
| T04 | cold unknown不當zero、worker async load、不可覆寫pending；public query不建立帳戶 | internal loader狀態與store類按現有設計最小加入 |
| T05 | authority balance/version同receipt、staleSQL不回退 | core-owned immutable receipt，不要求newframework |
| T06 | NOT_SENT／明確REJECTED／SENT_UNKNOWN可區分；unknown禁止blind ADD | typed outcome或exception、op marker、版本2script候選；新marker/TTL需ADR證明必要 |
| T07 | failed/rejectedtransfer無funds loss、durable補償或零單腿、雙帳戶lockorder | paired envelope/state machine/paired SQL方案以reproducer與backend證據選，不必全面替代pipeline |
| T08 | read不先clear、confirmed step重試不重做money、普通shutdown保留pending | 新spool/checkpoint可以採；若擴格式才migration/rollback fixture |
| T12 | core不依platform/Redis/JDBC、ports不反向依adapters | 冻結只限該task已選最小interface，不一次產生全部candidate類 |

T03/T05/T06選定的immutable DTO與ports住core，sync/storage只實作；JDK Clock足夠。每task在開始修改前將 exact signature、path、owner與consumer列於Interfaces/update log，review後才實作；不得建立無caller的新type。設計證據不足時提交ADR-07，不把candidate改名為Required便開工。

server-side operationId只追蹤internal work，不是caller idempotency key。stage與memory projected／authority confirmed／persisted三者分開；任一generic network error不能推論明確拒絕。marker缺失可能已執行（eviction/restart/expiry），SENT_UNKNOWN隔離，不因TTL內便blindretry。

若採slot reservation：未commit close release、commit後worker receipt及必要persistence成功才ack release；retry保留；quarantine只有持久記錄確認成功才release，重複ack不double-release。若採pending replay：保留confirmed base＋有序pending、SET barrier、transfer雙entry一致；safe rebuild無法證明就UNAVAILABLE／保留證據。以上是conditional candidate約束，不為未公開mutation finality建新global engine。
## Release priorities與task拆分規則

[P0] Confirmed release-blocking correctness issue；[P0-CANDIDATE] Suspected correctness issue awaiting evidence；[P1] Required 1.4 architecture/foundation；[P2] evidence/cost-supported desired improvement；[P3] optional/follow-up/experimental。

T01與T02–T08/T15目前標[P0-CANDIDATE]：它們是triage/最小修復工作包，不表示所有疑點confirmed。T01產生08 Evidence register四種結果；CONFIRMED才升對應issue為[P0]。Mixed task拆issue級priority：必要architecture部分[P1]，未證明必要的redesign[P2]，被否定的repair關閉而不實作。Task依賴表示先取得triage/interface交接，DISPROVED不要求虛構code fix。
- T03/T05/T06/T07/T08：observable safety contract保留，只有confirmed違反才P0；全新state store/paired envelope/new spool/marker framework屬P2候選，只有最小修復無法通過fault gate才提出ADR。
- T09：P1 additive upgrade與failure-safe metadata；若再現DDL/backup loss升該case為P0。
- T13：P1 existing owner/freshness/echo保留與adapter提取；global clock/version redesign P2／跨wire domain P3；確認money問題才P0。
- T16：P1 backend/SSE/auth與已知安全例外；完整WebSocket P3。
- T17：P1必要shared fixtures，不要求全部fake framework先完成；額外generic simulation P2。
- T19：P1 baseline/report；p99/alloc/throughput review可核准例外，不是可凌駕correctness的hard gate。
- T20：P3 optional，不加入release critical path。Public mutation與其idempotency全面contract同P3，不能借T11進SDK。

## Task通用執行／交接規則

每task固定四步：寫具體 failing/reproducer tests → 用指定command驗證失敗原因 → 最小修改或搬移 → 同command與requiredgate驗證成功。對candidate先triage，只有必要repair才寫code；DISPROVED附反證，INCONCLUSIVE/DEFERRED記review與後續。不能寫「適當測試」代替以下assertions。不存在futuretask時先建立task，不能把測試skip作綠燈。task輸出report含sourceSHA/command/results/changedfiles/remainingrisk。

實作開始可啟用依明確DAG的agents：一人ownerstate/receipt，一人compatfixture，一人platform，一人review；interface相關T03–T07順序交付。未知需求先讀現有code，發現無法滿足非blocking+finality時記ADR並停dependenttask，继续獨立fixture。

### T00 [P1] — Freeze現況與真正release baseline

**Required references:** 04§4；01/02/03中與manifest相應節；06 V00。其餘只在新證據需要時擴讀。

**Depends:** 無。**Files:** create `syncmoney-testkit/src/testFixtures/resources/compat/1.3.2/manifest.md`（初期尚未module可先fixture目录）；future dev report於ignored `build/reports/foundation/`。

**Interfaces:** consumes git/tag/artifacts；produces baselineSHA、descriptor/signature/key/Lua/config/SQLmanifest。不要將真userDB/config抄進fixtures。

- [x] Run `git status --short --branch`、`git log -12 --oneline`、`git tag --list '*1.3.2*'`，查verifiedtagcommit；分開HEAD71cc55d與tag。tag不存在則記可取得baseline而非虛構正式tag。
- [x] 依當前task閱讀必要implementation、callers/tests/config與相關audit節；T00建立area manifest／待驗證列表，不要求每個後續Agent逐次讀全repo。記exactsource/line、static/reproduced狀態；source drift再核對。
- [x] 在獨立disposablecheckout跑既有 Gradle/frontend gate（06 V00）；zip/jarresourcesinventory確認pluginmain、`player_uuid`、config12與artifact names。
- [x] Exit：manifest每個contract有sourceSHA，baselineactualfailures有分類；沒有當前工作樹reset或覆寫。

Shared-mode shutdown follow-up (commit `8326daa`): `EconomyEventConsumer.shutdown()` closes shared queue admission before stopping; the worker now remains active while `EconomyWriteQueue.hasOutstandingWrites()` is true, including reservations not yet committed to the event queue. The regression verifies new reservations are rejected after shutdown begins and a pre-shutdown reservation can still commit and be processed. RED evidence: without admission closure it failed the closure assertion; with closure but the old loop condition it observed the worker stop while the accepted reservation remained outstanding. Both failures and the final 32/32 focused gate ran under JDK 25. This does not add retry, quarantine, recovery, wire, or public API behavior.

### T05 execution record — 2026-10-07

- Status: COMPLETE; first dependency-ready task after T04. Baseline branch `linco/syncmoney-1.4.0-architecture-foundation-t00`, HEAD `b540cc6` before T05 changes.
- Workflow ruling: the installed plan helper only extracts headings named `Task N`, while this SSOT uses `T05`; `task-start`/`task-done` helpers are absent. The `task-brief` attempt correctly returned “task 5 not found”. The exact `### T05` section was manually extracted to the ignored SDD workspace; its required gates will be run and recorded directly. Cost if wrong: only the helper convention differs; task text remains verbatim from this plan.
- Interface freeze before implementation: add minimal `syncmoney-core/src/main/java/noietime/syncmoney/core/port/ConfirmedAccount.java` (`ConfirmedAccount(UUID playerUuid, BigDecimal balance, long version)`) and `ReplicationReceipt.java` (`ReplicationReceipt(String operationId, List<ConfirmedAccount> accounts)`). `CacheManager.atomicAddBalance(UUID, BigDecimal)` and `atomicSetBalance(UUID, BigDecimal)` return the exact authority-confirmed account. `EconomyEventConsumer` wraps it in a receipt and uses it for publish, DB enqueue, and accepted-write completion; retry state retains the same receipt. `DbWriteQueue.DbWriteTask(String operationId, ConfirmedAccount account, String playerName, String serverName, Instant timestamp)` preserves the confirmed pair through the existing queue.
- Storage interface: minimal `syncmoney-storage/src/main/java/noietime/syncmoney/storage/persistence/PlayerSnapshot.java` carries a `ConfirmedAccount` plus non-economic metadata. `SqlSnapshotWriter.persistPlayers(Connection, List<PlayerSnapshot>)` is called by `DatabaseManager` inside its existing batch transaction; `persistLocalBalance(Connection, UUID, String, BigDecimal, int, long)` is called by `LocalEconomyHandler` inside its current SQLite transaction. Storage depends on core; the root plugin depends on both. Root Bukkit lifecycle and active source sets remain in place. T12/T14 own later integration/extraction; duplicate active classes are prohibited.
- Necessary scaffold ruling: `syncmoney-core` is absent although T05 requires core-owned receipt DTOs and storage cannot depend on the Bukkit root without a cycle. T05 will add only this minimal Java module and the storage persistence module; it will not extract existing production classes or change public APIs, data/config schemas, or Redis protocol.
- ADR-14 decision: stale versions do not update balance or metadata; equal version/equal balance may refresh only non-economic metadata; equal version/different balance is a hard conflict and rolls back the transaction. Evidence gate: SQLite local format plus real PostgreSQL, MySQL, and MariaDB writers.
- Pre-implementation discovery: current shared `DatabaseManager` upserts unconditionally; `EconomyEventConsumer` drops Lua's returned version and separately reads cached version for publish, SQL, and queue completion. `LocalEconomyHandler` has unconditional SQLite upserts, with REAL balance and int version that must remain unchanged.

### T05 completion — 2026-10-07

- Confirmed behavior: atomic Redis results carry the balance and version returned by the same write into Pub/Sub, memory reconciliation, and SQL queue receipts. `/pay`, consumer writes, and direct Vault atomic transfers preserve each account pair; the Vault production setter path now passes its sync/config dependencies into the transfer handler.
- SQL behavior: newer snapshots cannot be overwritten by an older new-writer snapshot; equal version/equal balance is idempotent and may refresh only metadata; equal version/different balance raises a conflict and rolls back the batch. The existing SQLite `REAL` balance and `int` version representation is retained with a stale-version guard.
- ADR-14 remains the scoped rule. No schema, 1.3.2 writer, public API, Redis wire format, new engine, or recovery format was introduced. The integration fixture deliberately proves an old unconditional 1.3.2-style writer can still regress a row during mixed-version operation; the quiesced-upgrade limitation remains.
- Review: independent SQL review found no actionable issue in dialect, lock-wait, batch rollback, or legacy-writer fixtures. Receipt review's direct-Vault findings were repaired by applying both confirmed accounts to memory and queueing both snapshots. A separate setter-path regression failed before dependency forwarding and passed after it; targeted independent re-review found no remaining finding. No code-review item remains open for T05.
- Validation: root receipt/SQL/cross-server/Vault/pay focused gate **33/33 passed**; real SQL integration **1/1 passed** against PostgreSQL 18.1, MySQL 8.0.42, and MariaDB 12.3.3; `shadowJar`, PAPI jar, and `acceptanceJar` succeeded. `:syncmoney-storage:test` is `NO-SOURCE` (no unit-test source set); SQLite stale-write coverage ran in root tests. Full root suite: **301 tests, 1 failure**, only `TransferCompensationTest.receiverCancellationRestoresDurableSender` (E02/T07); this is not an aggregate PASS and no T05-focused failure occurred. Logs are ignored under `build/reports/foundation/T05-*`.
- NOT RUN: PlugDev/Paper/Vault runtime, live Redis Lua, Folia/Canvas, crash recovery, and mixed-version deployment. E05 queue-saturation/overflow replay semantics remain assigned to T06/T08; T05 does not claim queue-full recovery or old-writer safety.

Recursive deletion of the test-only Temp directories was rejected by the tool safety gate; the stopped Temp files are retained. The system PostgreSQL 5432 and MySQL 3306 services were not stopped or modified.

### T06 completion — 2026-10-07

- Status: COMPLETE for T06's assigned E05 Redis mutation and retry semantics. Baseline was `a11202e` on `linco/syncmoney-1.4.0-architecture-foundation-t00`; implementation commit: `3b85b61`. T07 is next under the DAG.
- Lua add/set/transfer now use decimal-string cents arithmetic and preflight all key types, SQL `DECIMAL(20,2)` balance bounds, and signed `BIGINT` version overflow before the first mutation. Lua returns version strings via `GET` after `INCR`, preserving values above `2^53`; the existing `NEGATIVE_BALANCE` error code remains compatible.
- `CacheManager` and `/pay` distinguish `NOT_SENT`, `REJECTED`, and `SENT_UNKNOWN`. Confirmed Redis receipt pairs flow together; known no-mutation rejections do not retry. Unknown accepted consumer events are quarantined in-process. `/pay` validates receipts and retains in-flight, unknown, and post-receipt-failure protection state per sender; Redis recovering after startup degradation lazily loads the retained transfer-script source.
- No operation marker was selected. A lost Redis response therefore remains ambiguous and is quarantined rather than blindly replaying a delta; this is not exactly-once behavior or durable quarantine. T08 owns restart recovery. Direct Vault atomic-transfer unknown-outcome caller handling remains with T07 under E02.

**Interfaces/update log (2026-10-09, before implementation):** `syncmoney-core` owns `core.state.AccountState(BigDecimal balance, long version, long lastAccessTime)`, `core.state.AccountStateStore` (UUID keyed account snapshots, atomic map operations, and stable UUID ordered `lockAccounts`/`tryLockAccount` lease), `core.money.MoneyPolicy` (existing HALF_UP scale-2 normalization and balance bound), and `core.admission.WriteAdmission` (`tryAcquire`, timed `acquire`, idempotent lease close). Consumers are the existing `MemoryStateManager`, `EconomyFacade`, `TransactionWriter`, `TransferOrchestrator`, `EconomyWriteQueue`, and compatibility `NumericUtil`/`Constants`; these existing owners remain responsible for adapters, event/recovery policy, logging, and lifecycle. `EconomyFacade.EconomyState`, `TransferContext`, and `PendingRollback` retain their existing nested public binary shape; state manager maps core snapshots internally and converts only at the legacy public accessor. No new Redis, SQL, CMI, public SDK, or persistence contract is introduced. Ruling: extract the small pure primitives used by the current engine and keep adapter-heavy reconciliation in its Bukkit owner; cost if wrong: state policy may remain less centralized than a future core-only engine, but this avoids moving third-party lifecycle/storage semantics or breaking legacy record descriptors during this task.

It adds no SDK/API package, Redis key, wire field, sequencing, or compare-and-set behavior. See ADR-16.

### T13 execution record — 2026-10-09

- Two independent source reviews found the same P1 pair-coherence gap: split balance/version GETs could let join reconciliation associate an old balance with a new version and mark the version applied; split outbound SETs could tear the pair. This is a source-level interleaving, not a reproduced live Redis money loss, so it is not promoted to P0.
- **Ruling:** interpret 04 §12's paired mirror requirement as atomic snapshot and atomic pair replacement using existing keys (ADR-16). Do not add CAS/SET-Lua, a new key, wire field, public SDK type, global sequence, or persistence contract; these remain deferred. Cost if wrong: restricted Redis ACLs that permit GET/SET but deny MGET/MSET could reject CMI reconciliation/writes; live ACL/runtime compatibility is not established. Redis Cluster remains outside this task's supported contract.

The first GREEN attempt exposed a stale test that expected transaction SET calls; the characterization was updated to assert MSET and retain the deferred old-message overwrite behavior.
- T13 independent re-review found no actionable issue after the paired adapter change. It was source-only; it did not run tests or a live Redis/CMI server.
- `CmiOwnerFreshnessTest.expiredWatermarkAllowsAnOlderInboundVersionAgain` confirms the bounded watermark permits an old version after expiry. A reviewer additionally noted that conflicting equal-version callbacks after eviction/expiry are not directly characterized; this remains a P2 hypothesis because no licensed CMI/runtime evidence establishes a live unsafe mutation. Do not describe it as disproved or P0.
- E07 remains P1. E08 remains INCONCLUSIVE / P0-CANDIDATE and outside mandatory correctness scope.
- T13 code and required local evidence are committed in `6aa85fc` (implementation/tests) and `64a2b42` (documentation); overall T13 remains **BLOCKED by V05**. Sequencing ruling (2026-10-09): T14's `Depends` field requires T13's necessary CMI contract and assembly prerequisites, not live CMI acceptance. The new `syncmoney-bukkit` module owns platform/runtime Java and tests while canonical resources and root aliases remain stable. `DbWriteQueue` is in storage; `PubSubMessage` and `CMIVersioning` are in sync; public package names/descriptors are preserved. `verifyArchitecture` checks resolved compile/runtime project and external-dependency boundaries across production modules. The sync Redis integration source set has only test-scoped storage/Bukkit/Hikari/Vault dependencies. No ADR or public product contract changed.

- Fresh JDK 25.0.4 / Gradle 9.8.0 full matrix passed: API 3/3, core 10/10, protocol 15/15, storage 8/8, sync 9/9, Redis integration 29/29, Bukkit 457/457, PAPI 5/5; zero failures/errors/skips. `shadowJar`, PAPI JAR, API consumer JAR, acceptance JAR, aggregated JaCoCo reports, `preparePlugdev`, `verifyArchitecture`, and `verifyPackaging` succeeded. Exact command/output: ignored `build/reports/foundation/T14-final-validation-jdk25.log`.
- JAR inspection: 9,447 entries / 8,575 classes / zero duplicate classes / zero testkit, benchmark, acceptance-probe or JMH classes; 42 embedded web entries; application classfile major 69. Public SDK has exactly three ADR-03 classes. API consumer JAR has zero SDK classes. Root output SHA-256 and `build/plugdev/Syncmoney.jar` SHA-256 both equal `8940BA5B9F0D87115CCF4EDEDBD47AAB0DBAA0A0BB71D85E4EA40A915B44C703`. This output retains the checkout's default `1.3.2` filename and is not the released 1.3.2 JAR.
- `javap -public -s` on the three SDK classes is identical for the frozen alpha API JAR and current module JAR; both API JAR SHA-256 values are `688BCD67DD9D1AE988BB2FCBB2EE16A55CCADFC3659F5E2D95EED01290FAE0D4`. The compiled 1.3.2 binary consumer links the legacy Syncmoney getters and PubSub/CMI methods. Both plugin/PAPI cross-pairs pass all 10 reflection seams; logs are `T14-compat-final-legacy.log`, `T14-compat-final-old-papi-new-plugin.log`, and `T14-compat-final-new-papi-old-plugin.log`.

### T15 [P0-CANDIDATE] — Lifecycle、disabled与entityownership

**Status:** COMPLETE (2026-10-09, final scheduler repair). T14 is complete; T13 licensed CMI V05 remains an independent T21/release gate and is not waived by T15.

- [x] 每initstagefail→已创建handles逆序关且各异常独立；disabledfeatureszeroexecutor/schema/folder/http/subscriptions；perplayerguard独立globalbreaker。
- [x] logout/teleportdeadline只cleartracking不移queue；livecallbackretirement取消；globalcollectionsnapshot后entitydispatch、teleportAsync保cause/destination。
- [x] producerstop後newAPI/Vaultreject；queue/retry/drain時DB/Redis/audit仍live；closeauditthrow不阻storageclose。

T15 preflight (2026-10-09, source SHA `a0e7e7b1bd42b205b6bafe2dd197a3f007d92aa8`): tracked worktree was clean on `linco/syncmoney-1.4.0-architecture-foundation-t00`. Source review found missing per-owner disable isolation; economy provider unregister/quiescence before drain; cancellation/fencing for delayed Vault and VaultUnlocked registration; unregistering event handlers before persistence drain; and retained cancellation handles for version refresh/cache cleanup schedules. These gaps are closed in this task with owner-scoped shutdown steps, provider/listener/task lifecycle fences, and focused regression tests. `EconomyWriteQueue.closeAdmission()` remains the accepted-write boundary and preserves pre-close reservations.

T15 final closure (2026-10-09): the final scheduler review finding was fixed in the acceptance fixture: plugin, Vault service, and destination-world lookups run on the global region scheduler; account readiness polling runs async; player writes, state checks, kick, and teleport run on the player entity scheduler. The original fixture and test evidence were refreshed rather than reused. Full current results and exact artifacts are recorded in 06: the full Gradle gate passed 48 actionable tasks / 563 tests with no failures, errors, or skips; the isolated Paper `1.21.6#48` LOCAL_REDIS run observed two accepted writes, deferred `teleportAsync` during a 2500 ms Redis pause, eventual Nether arrival, two further accepted writes before logout, and final Redis `4.00/version 4` after graceful server stop. The targeted reviewer re-review found no remaining actionable scheduler finding. The 12 platform/profile smoke matrix covers the user-confirmed lowest pins Paper `1.21.6#48`, Folia `1.21.6#6`, Canvas `1.21.8#603` and highest smoke pins Paper `26.3#166`, Folia `26.2#7`, Canvas `26.3#972`; these are startup/provider/profile smokes and do not establish player entity behavior on Folia or Canvas. No live multi-server SQL/Redis or hard-crash behavior is claimed. Licensed CMI V05 remains BLOCKED/NOT RUN because no authorized runtime JAR is available and is still a T21/release gate. E07 remains P1; E08 remains INCONCLUSIVE/P0-CANDIDATE and outside mandatory correctness fixes. No public API, config/schema, wire, or recovery format was added. T16 is next under the existing task DAG.

**Required references:** 04§19；06 V01/V02；需實作的fake port契約。其餘只在新證據需要時擴讀。

**Status:** COMPLETE (2026-10-09; implementation commit `26e0fb7`).
**Preflight ruling (2026-10-09):** T12 explicitly retained only caller-backed core primitives and rejected unused candidate types; current `syncmoney-core` has no account-repository or replication interfaces. Keep the fake port contract in the `syncmoney-testkit` test-fixture variant and validate its version/error/idempotency behavior against existing storage/Redis semantics, without adding production core ports or runtime dependencies. Cost if wrong: test-only contracts could drift from a later production port; mitigate with reusable conformance tests against available real adapters.

**Interfaces:**fake实现coreports，controls `failBeforeCommit/failAfterCommit/dropResponse/deliverDuplicate/deliverOutOfOrder/retireEntity`；JDKClock可ManualClock subclass。

- [x] Exit：productionruntime不含testkit，concurrencetests不用Thread.sleep猜timing。

Performance interpretation caveat: independent review found the synthetic current/legacy receipt consumers do not perform identical completion bookkeeping. The t=2 report remains bound observed evidence for its named profile, but it does not isolate writer-only cost; see 06 T19 performance review before making causal claims.

- **T8 disposition (historical review; open-loop gates later closed):** accepted the fresh 8-producer run's report, manifest, marker, 192-row matrix, and trial postconditions; its byte-identical measurement result is not asserted against t=2 because the benchmark JAR/source-tree hashes differ after report-tooling changes. Throughput passed 27/48, allocation 12/48, and SampleTime p99 18/48 matched-case thresholds. No release performance waiver is approved. The component JMH suites are closed and schema-2 Redis/SQL macro pilot r4 is accepted as calibration input. The open-loop final outcome is recorded below and in 06; detailed provenance and performance limits remain in 06.

**Depends:** T14＋測量所需test fixtures（T17相關部分）；不等待Central T18。**Files:** benchmarks Gradle/JMH/runner/profiles；futuretestreportignored。

**Interfaces:**`benchmarkJar`输出runner、`runBaselineComparison -PbaselineCheckout=<verified isolated path>`；profiles+resultJSONmetadata schema见06。

- [x] baselineharness对旧facade采用test-timeadapter且保留真实checks；不能把oldasyncoperation当newcommittedlatency来比。
- [x] JMH balance/deposit/withdraw/set/transfer/queue/codec/message-apply and current-only Queue/ProtocolCodec/MessageApply/SQLite snapshot suites; capacity and data reset stay outside measured operations.
- [x] Fresh schema-2 Redis/SQL baseline macro pilot r4 accepted as calibration input against the verified disposable backends; receipt and provenance are recorded in 06.
- [x] Ran the checked-in 60-scenario open-loop matrix against the verified disposable Redis/SQL backends; all 60 unique tuples passed report, sample, correctness, drain, cleanup, and independent empty-backend gates (full evidence in 06).
- [x] Paired and current-only SampleTime rows meet the applicable 100,000-sample minimum; offered/accepted/rejected/persisted counters are separated, allocation/GC is recorded, and benchmark-specific correctness postconditions remain active.
- [x] Recorded open-loop arrival/response, accepted-receipt/durable latency, bounded admission rejections, and backlog/repair and funds/version invariants from all 60 scenarios; no authority rejection, ambiguity, quarantine, version conflict, or money invariant failure occurred.
- [x] Paired/current-only commands and report provenance are recorded in 06 B00; reports contain no credentials. Fresh post-Actor Gradle root/architecture/packaging gate passed.
- [x] Independently confirmed macro-pilot and open-loop report schema, source/profile/environment provenance, money/version checks, drain, cleanup, and post-run backend emptiness before accepting the results.
- [x] Exit: recorded observed results without prefilled benefit claims and calibrated the benchmark from the accepted baseline pilot; final release performance disposition stays with T22.

### T21 [P1] — Upgrade、downgrade与mixed矩阵

**Required references:** 04§13/14/15；06 V03/M00–M12；08 ADR-01/04/07。其餘只在新證據需要時擴讀。

**Depends:** 必要T09/T13/T14/T15/T16與已確認P0；T19 performance review支線獨立，可並行。T20與未批准redesign不是前置。**Files:** PlugDevfutureupgradehelper、syntheticfixtures、operatorrunbookdocs两語。

**Interfaces:**06 M00–M12；resultsseparatestatic/wire/live/fault/skip。

- [x] M04 LOCAL SQLite fixture→1.4 normal-drain→1.3.2 rollback subcase: PASS on all six user-supplied endpoint builds, historical supplemental Paper `26.3#175`, and refreshed current-stable Paper `26.3#178`; the minimum Paper `1.21.6#48` runner-regression rerun also passed after limiting expected API-consumer failure to 1.3.2 stages. LOCAL's `local-persistence.journal` was absent after each normal 1.4 stop. The bounded ADR-15 aggregate downgrade subgate now also passes: existing M01–M03 SYNC/PostgreSQL+Redis rollback reports cover all six user-supplied endpoints, with a validated zero-pending candidate spool and unchanged spool state after 1.3.2 rollback; direct pending-spool refusal coverage was added to the recovery validator test. This closes only the exercised mode/backend path, not the remaining T21 matrix; see 06 T21 M04 aggregate downgrade gate.
- [x] M05 guarded SQL snapshot integration: PASS on isolated PostgreSQL 18.1, MySQL 8.0.42, and MariaDB 12.3.3; result and review evidence are in 06 V03/M05. Synthetic schema IDs were preserved. This does not claim `DatabaseManager` startup or schema-migration coverage.
- [x] M05 baltop SQL identifier quoting subcase: PASS on minimum Paper `1.21.6#48` / Java 25 across MySQL 8.0.42, MariaDB 12.3.3, and PostgreSQL 18.1; dialect quoting preserves the existing `rank` column and SQL/Redis balances and versions matched. This closes only the bounded baltop DDL/upsert path; see 06 T21 M05 runtime evidence.
- [x] M05 minimum-Paper MySQL SYNC runtime cell: PASS_BOUNDED_SUBCASE on Paper `1.21.6#48`, isolated MySQL `8.0.42` and Redis `8.10.1`; two Vault deposits, normal drain/restart reads, and SQL/Redis balance/version parity passed. The final report is `mysql-sync-minimum-paper-20261013-142006-3403e55238784f6f89f96fef050230c7/result.json`; its hash and strict offline audit are recorded in 06. This adds one database-startup/write/read cell only; the remaining Paper/Folia/Canvas, SQL-dialect, mode/resource, and failure matrix remains open.
- [x] M05 minimum-Paper MySQL legacy-data read/rollback compatibility: PASS_BOUNDED_SUBCASE (E2) on Paper `1.21.6#48`, Java 25.0.4, MySQL `8.0.42`, and Redis `8.10.1`; 1.3.2-seeded balances and versions remained unchanged through 1.4 startup and 1.3.2 rollback. At schema version 11, 1.4 additively repaired three missing audit indexes without changing the 15 audit columns or version row; rollback retained the indexes. ### T03 [P0-CANDIDATE] COMPLETE — approved E03/E06 admission and pending-ownership scope (2026-10-06; CMI source-quiescence rule resolved in ADR-11)
- Owner/worktree: `/root`, branch `linco/syncmoney-1.4.0-architecture-foundation-t00`; started from T02 commit `f1dd7162c6060aba3e69bc9b7884c1949ed89c61`. T03 implementation and regression changes are committed as b8867c8.
- E03 repair: reserve bounded shared-write capacity before account mutation; commit the event while applying the state transition; a full queue rejects before balance/version/post-event changes. Accepted ownership remains through queue polling and releases only when consumer work completes. LOCAL writes retain their existing persistence path.
- E06 repair: accepted writes protect their memory entry from expiry/capacity eviction and remote updates; only strictly newer remote versions apply. Background version refresh now uses monotonic update acceptance rather than force replacement. Migration force updates require the maintenance lock and no pending accepted write.
- Migration lock decision (user, 2026-10-05): while `MigrationLock` is active, reject all Syncmoney external economy writes, including `ADMIN_SET` and `COMMAND_ADMIN`. The CMI migration worker receives one thread-scoped, one-shot permit for its own `ADMIN_SET`; synchronous reentrant listener writes remain rejected. A saturated shared queue makes `MigrationTask` fail that player before checkpoint/SQL/Redis side effects.
- CMI source-quiescence ruling (ADR-11; user delegated technical decision authority 2026-10-06): configured CMI economy commands and module are disabled before the source snapshot and remain disabled after success. Snapshot begins only after the reload command is accepted by Bukkit; this is not proof that arbitrary third-party direct API or database writers are stopped. Operators must quiesce all external CMI source writers for the migration window. A detected non-echo balance event invalidates the snapshot, prevents further migration permits, marks the checkpoint failed, and prevents completion. Partial target data is not activated; recover by quiescing source writers and starting a fresh migration with `-force`. Because CMI reports balance changes after its authority has already changed, Syncmoney cannot veto arbitrary direct API calls. This is an explicit operational precondition, not a claim that external writers are technically fenced.
- CMI outbound mirror behavior: a denied mirror write remains retryable while `MigrationLock` is held; the CMI listener records its last-known mirror balance only after a successful `trySyncAbsoluteBalance`. Unidentifiable non-echo balance events conservatively dirty the active snapshot. Actual CMI runtime behavior remains NOT RUN.
- E06 review discovery/fix (2026-10-06): the consumer can pair an earlier Redis write balance with a later cached version. If a newer remote snapshot is deferred at that version, completion now keeps the deferred balance on a version tie instead of synthesizing a newer-version receipt from the stale balance. `MemoryPendingStateTest.deferredRemoteBalanceWinsWhenCompletionReceiptCarriesItsVersion` failed before the change and passed after it. This is a minimum internal behavior correction; it adds no storage/wire format or supported third-party integration API contract.
- Terminal retry ownership discovery (2026-10-06): after maximum retries, the consumer removes the event from its retry map but retains its accepted reservation because `OverflowLogInterface.log` has no durable-ack result; `RedisOverflowLog` can fall back to stderr. This is fail-closed: the unresolved account remains protected and each terminal event can retain one bounded admission slot until restart/recovery or manual reconciliation. Do not release it without durable-recovery evidence; overflow acknowledgment/recovery semantics remain outside T03 and with the existing T08 work.
- Latest focused validation: JDK 25.0.4; root `:test` filtered to `AdmissionReservationTest`, `MemoryPendingStateTest`, `QueueAdmissionFailureTest`, `EconomyEventConsumerTest`, `MigrationTaskAdmissionTest`, `MigrationTaskLockTest`, `PayTransferMigrationLockTest`, `CMIEconomyMigrationLockTest`, `EconomyModeRouterMigrationLockTest`, `TransferMigrationLockTest`, and `CMIPubsubMigrationLockTest`, with `--rerun-tasks` — **47 tests, 0 failures/errors/skips; exit 0**. Per-class counts and command are in 06.
- Latest full root regression: `./gradlew.bat :test --rerun-tasks` — **187 tests, 4 assertion failures, 0 errors/skips; exit 1**. Failures remain `ConsumerFailureSemanticsTest.postRedisFailureDoesNotApplyDeltaTwice` (E05/T06-T08), `ConsumerFailureSemanticsTest.shutdownRetriesFailedDownstreamWorkWithoutReapplyingRedis` (E08 remains INCONCLUSIVE/P0-CANDIDATE and non-mandatory), `TransferCompensationTest.receiverCancellationRestoresDurableSender` (E02/T07), and `VaultTest.coldVaultReadReturnsZeroWhenRedisReportsZeroDespiteExistingSqlAccount` (T04/ADR-04). T03 focused E03/E06 and migration-lock regressions pass; the full root suite is not green.
- Limits: live Redis/SQL, real CMI migration, LOCAL SQLite migration, restart/crash recovery, Folia/Canvas, and PlugDev E03/E06 scenarios are NOT RUN. `:syncmoney-core:test` was NOT RUN because the module does not exist and creating a scaffold is outside the approved minimum repair. No claim of live shared-backend recovery or CMI source stability is made.
- Ruling: use the existing root `:test` source set instead of creating the plan's candidate `:syncmoney-core` scaffold — the user authorized the minimum owner-scoped correction and explicitly said proposed modules/models are not mandatory; a test-only module split is not needed to establish T03 behavior — cost if wrong: the exact proposed module-level command remains unexercised, while equivalent focused root tests and root regression were executed.
- Ruling: leave the one-leg transfer failure under capacity rejection for T07 — the user assigned E02 to T07 and T07 depends on T06; fixing paired admission in T03 would cross owner and skip the dependency — cost if wrong: shared-mode transfers may still debit the sender when the second leg is rejected until T07 closes E02; release stays blocked meanwhile.
- Reopened 2026-10-05 after independent adversarial review found three unresolved interleavings: migration lock activation raced with writers paused in callbacks; a strictly newer remote authority snapshot was discarded while writes were pending; capacity cleanup could remove an entry refreshed after the candidate snapshot. A separate final review also found `executeAtomicTransfer` bypassed the migration lock. These are being repaired within T03's E03/E06 and the user's migration-lock decision; E02/E05/E08 ownership remains unchanged.
- Current implementation adds a non-blocking write lease before transaction callbacks and mutations. Lock acquisition now succeeds only with no active producer; CMI and Local-to-Sync migrations stop before source reads/snapshots if acquisition fails. All facade transaction/transfer mutation entries, including direct atomic transfer paths, acquire the same gate. A no-source-row CMI exit releases the lock.
- Internal-surface ruling: `MigrationLock.WriteLease` and `tryAcquireWrite()` are public JVM members because existing economy, command, migration, and sync packages must share the same lease gate. They are not exported through a supported `syncmoney-api` contract or documented as third-party integration APIs; `consumeMigrationWriteAuthorization()` is private. No stable external API promise is added.
- E06 now retains the highest deferred remote snapshot while accepted writes are pending. On completion, the deferred snapshot takes precedence over an authority balance at the same version to preserve a matched balance/version pair. Capacity cleanup removes a sorted candidate only if its current immutable state is still the exact state selected by the snapshot.
- Task status: COMPLETE for the approved T03 E03/E06 scope plus ADR-11's bounded CMI source contract. JDK 25 focused admission/pending-state gates passed at 47/47; the latest migration gate passed 45/45, covering source identity persistence/legacy checkpoints, incomplete/error reads, failed row/SQL/Redis writes, dirty-permit races, and pre-snapshot CMI disable. No real CMI/Redis/SQL, Folia/Canvas, crash recovery, or PlugDev E03/E06 runtime matrix was run. The CMI event is post-change: arbitrary third-party API/SQL writers are not vetoed and must be quiesced operationally. On a detected mutation, migration aborts; partial target rows must be replaced by a new `-force` migration. T04 is COMPLETE and T05 is next under the existing DAG. E02/E05/E06 follow-up work remains with T07/T06/T08/T15. Independent E06 re-review closed the observed in-memory race; terminal retry reservation remains a documented fail-closed capacity limitation.
- Reopened 2026-10-06 after independent recovery/fencing review: a migration started with -force does not persist that policy, so resume runs with force=false and can skip positive target balances while advancing to completion; additionally, CMI source dirty recording can occur after the one-shot permit is consumed but before the balance mutation commits. Both findings are in T03's migration correctness boundary. Add RED regressions, persist force policy compatibly (legacy missing field means false), and serialize the source-dirty fence with the accepted memory mutation. T05 remains next by DAG and is paused only until these T03 repairs pass their focused and regression gates.
### T03 ADR-12 reopened repair closure — 2026-10-06

- Force-resume repair: the existing checkpoint now persists nullable `forceOverwrite`; an absent field in a legacy checkpoint resumes with `false`. A two-row SQLite resume regression verifies an original forced run still overwrites a positive target balance after resume. `MigrationCheckpointFailureTest` covers current and legacy checkpoint decoding.
- Dirty-source failure repair: migration memory mutation and post-change dirty marking share a short JVM gate. A dirty-first rejected row enters the terminal invalid-snapshot path before ordinary row-failure retry handling; the checkpoint becomes FAILED and cannot resume. If memory commit wins first, later source dirtiness is checked around SQL/Redis effects and at atomic completion; backend I/O never runs under the gate.
- `MigrationTaskLockTest.sourceMutationBeforeMigrationCommitFailsCheckpointInsteadOfAllowingResume` uses a two-row SQLite source. It migrates row one, then dirties CMI as row two is rejected at its memory commit. It failed before the fix because the checkpoint remained resumable and passed after routing the catch through `abortIfCmiSourceChanged()`.
- Final T03 focused gate, JDK 25.0.4 / Gradle 9.8.0: root `:test --rerun-tasks` filtered to all migration tests, `MigrationConfigTest`, admission/pending/consumer tests, migration-lock router/transfer tests, and `TransactionWriterOutcomeTest` — **87 tests, 0 failures/errors/skips; exit 0**. Full output: ignored `build/reports/foundation/T03-focused-final-after-adr12.log`.
- Fresh root regression `./gradlew.bat :test --rerun-tasks` — **277 tests, 1 assertion failure, 0 errors/skips; exit 1**. Sole failure: `TransferCompensationTest.receiverCancellationRestoresDurableSender`, owned by T07/E02. T03 failures: none. Full output: ignored `build/reports/foundation/T03-root-suite-after-adr12.log`.
- Independent review found the dirty-first row catch could pause and later resume after lease release cleared dirty state. The regression reproduced it; the fix makes the invalid snapshot terminal, and targeted re-review found no remaining issue. A second dirty-fence reviewer found no additional actionable finding in event→commit→SQL/Redis ordering.
- Real CMI runtime/reload and migration, live Redis/SQL, third-party CMI producer quiescence, crash recovery, Folia/Canvas, and PlugDev acceptance remain NOT RUN. The local PlugDev project has no usable CMI runtime JAR/profile. ADR-11 still requires the operator to quiesce arbitrary CMI API/SQL writers.
- **Task status: T03 COMPLETE** for approved E03/E06 behavior and the ADR-11/12/13 CMI migration contract. **T05 is now IN PROGRESS** as the next dependency-ready task. No new engine, standalone recovery format, or public contract was added.

### T03 source-content fingerprint follow-up — 2026-10-07

- Independent review found that a paused CMI checkpoint checked source identity and row count only; changing a balance while preserving row count could resume at an old offset and combine old target rows with new source content. T03 was reopened before T05 implementation.
- Engineering decision ADR-13 adds an optional `sourceFingerprint` to the existing checkpoint. It is SHA-256 over length-prefixed canonical migration-output fields (player name, parsed UUID or null, normalized balance); row count remains separately stored. Single-source fingerprint and page reads use the same SQL ordering. Multi-source candidates are canonically sorted before merge, and merged rows use the same canonical order for pagination and fingerprinting.
- Missing fingerprint in a legacy CMI checkpoint or changed content fails the checkpoint. Operators must quiesce CMI source writers and start a fresh `-force` migration to replace partial target rows. ADR-11's limit remains: arbitrary API/SQL writers cannot be vetoed; runtime CMI behavior is not claimed.
- TDD evidence: the same-count source-content resume regressions failed before the fingerprint guard (single-source and multi-source); `MigrationSourceFingerprintTest.multiSourceMergeTieIsStableWhenSqlRowOrderChanges` failed before canonical pre-merge ordering with different SHA-256 values for opposite SQLite insertion orders, then passed after the fix.
- Final T03 focused command: JDK 25.0.4, Gradle 9.8.0, all migration tests plus migration config, admission/pending/consumer and migration-lock router/transfer tests, with `--rerun-tasks` — **92 tests, 0 failures/errors/skips; exit 0**. Exact command and log are recorded in 06.
- Full root regression: **282 tests, 1 assertion failure, 0 errors/skips; exit 1**. Sole failure `TransferCompensationTest.receiverCancellationRestoresDurableSender` (E02/T07), where reload observes sender `90.00` and total supply `90.00` instead of `100.00`; this remains T07's assigned work, not a T03 failure.
- Review: first independent fingerprint review found unstable tie resolution before merge; the fix passed targeted re-review with no remaining finding. A separate independent source/resume correctness review also found no issue. Reviewers did not run tests or claim real CMI runtime coverage.
- Commits: `8aaf3b1` records the staged T03/T04 integration layer; `b540cc6` separately records the T03 source-content fingerprint and deterministic merge follow-up.
- NOT RUN: real CMI runtime/reload/migration, arbitrary third-party CMI API/SQL write fence, live Redis/SQL, crash/restart or power-loss recovery, Folia/Canvas, and PlugDev migration acceptance. Evidence is local E1 only for fingerprint and resume behavior.

ADR-03已依使用者委派的技術決策權限resolved：只公開read/query/service discovery，不含mutation、internal state、version或economy mode。ApiInfo/info()移除，因repository沒有第三方diagnostics use case，且ServicesManager、plugin descriptor及artifact coordinates已提供所需discovery/version。CMI cold read僅在online entity owner上安全查詢；offline/unloaded且無local snapshot回exceptional unknown，不宣稱absence且不建立CMI user。T11完成後publication仍須T18/T22/T23 gates。

Redis authority rejection rebuilds pending state from its confirmed base; an ambiguous result remains quarantined under T06 semantics.

- 2026-10-10 18:31:21 +08:00 T19 read-only discovery: source inspection confirms that the live consumers poll an event from EconomyWriteQueue before processing it, while completeOwnedWrite unconditionally calls removeQueuedIdentity and that helper runs queue.removeIf by object identity. Thus ordinary completed events can trigger a full scan of the remaining backlog even though the consumer already dequeued them. This is a plausible mechanism for the active-run drain failures, not a confirmed root cause or measured production impact. r1 remains active at 54.86%; no source changes or new measurements were made.
- 2026-10-10 18:32:23 +08:00 T19 independent read-only review: reviewer confirmed the timeout is a slow-drain teardown failure, not evidence of a stuck thread or observed funds/version corruption. The benchmark adapter and production event consumer both poll before calling complete; completion then identity-scans the remaining LinkedBlockingQueue via removeIf. The 10-second trial teardown limit exposes that cost. Reviewer disposition: do not accept r1; after PID 25200 exits, run a focused current/uniform/SET/10,000-account, two-thread, one-fork B00 diagnostic with drainer CPU/stack capture. No code edits or overlapping measurements while r1 remains active.

- 2026-10-10 T19 report-validator integration follow-up: the first current-only quick smoke completed its JMH trial checks but Gradle 9.8 failed the report phase because `Project.javaexec()` is unavailable. Replaced both report-tool calls with `ProviderFactory.exec`; a second smoke then exposed the actual JMH 1.37 SampleTime JSON shape: `primaryMetric.rawData` is absent and `rawDataHistogram` is partitioned as `[fork][measurementIteration][bucket]`. This is corroborated by the accepted 192-row t=1 artifact (3 forks × 10 SampleTime histograms per fork). Added a regression test that first failed with the missing-rawData error; the validator now validates throughput rawData by fork/iteration and SampleTime histograms by fork/iteration. Focused validator suite passed 9/9. Current-only quick report smoke passed with 2/2 expected rows and manifest byte/SHA binding; its sample count is below 100,000, so no p99.9 confidence interval is emitted. This is integration/report-shape evidence only, not performance evidence. T19 remains IN_PROGRESS; rerun full t=2 matrix after independent review. User-provided runtime matrix was checked against T15: minimum Paper 1.21.6#48 / Folia 1.21.6#6 / Canvas 1.21.8#603; highest smoke Paper 26.3#166 / Folia 26.2#7 / Canvas 26.3#972.
- 2026-10-10 T19 report-harness follow-up: a reviewer finding showed the quick manifest could accept PowerShell error text as cpuModel and preserve a mojibake power-plan display string. Replaced `-Command` with UTF-16LE `-EncodedCommand`, set UTF-8 output and silenced startup progress, separated stdout/stderr and rejected stderr/nonzero exits, recorded only the power-plan GUID, and made host-provenance validation reject multiline/oversized CPU values or malformed captured GUIDs. Two added regressions first failed (2 failures) and the complete focused suite now passes 12/12. A second current-only quick smoke on JMH 1.37 / JDK 25.0.4 / 2 producers passed 2/2 rows; its manifest records CPU `Intel(R) Core(TM) i9-10900K CPU @ 3.70GHz`, power-plan GUID `381b4222-f694-41f0-9685-ff5bb260df2e`, 37,024 result bytes, SHA-256 `8fab32916a1c7fd37f267fe2637d42cce560e97997fc968f06e8dba0a175458e`, and 7,667 SampleTime samples with no p99.9 interval. The prior quick smoke manifest with contaminated CPU provenance is superseded for host facts. Both report-hardening and host-provenance repairs received independent targeted review with no remaining findings. The focused t=2 drain diagnostic completed with accepted=offered and zero rejects but sampled `removeQueuedIdentity` once; it did not reproduce the six 10-second teardown timeouts, so the fresh full t=2 matrix remains required. T19 remains IN_PROGRESS; next: commit this benchmark-only report hardening, rerun t=2, and retain the existing t=8 gate.

- 2026-10-10 T19 repair hypothesis / engineering decision: track queued-vs-in-flight events in a bounded identity set maintained at commit and poll boundaries; `removeQueuedIdentity` will skip `LinkedBlockingQueue.removeIf` once a worker has dequeued the event, while still removing events completed/cancelled before poll. This is an internal fast path, preserves the current queue, bounded admission, and public contract, and avoids scanning every backlog item on ordinary completion. Verify with a real `LinkedBlockingQueue` instrumentation test that counts examined elements; do not modify production code until that regression fails against the current implementation.


- 2026-10-10 T19 queue-repair follow-up: independent review found a dequeue/membership race; the barrier regression reproduced 2 remaining-backlog predicate inspections before the repair. A pre-interrupted-poll regression also failed against the first monitor draft, so polling now uses an interruptible ReentrantLock and Condition. Enqueue, dequeue plus identity-membership removal, and queued-event removal share that lock; timed condition waits release it. No queue engine or public contract changed. Targeted review after repair found no actionable issue. Full local gates passed: 651 tests, 0 failures/errors/skips, 59 Gradle tasks; architecture, packaging, benchmark isolation/provenance, and git diff --check passed. The fail-fast left Redis DB size=0 and SQL players rows=0. Ruling: retain the existing benchmark runtime dependency boundary and connect to MariaDB over the project-supported MySQL Connector/J jdbc:mysql: scheme; this is compatible with the same MariaDB 12.3.3 endpoint and does not add a production or benchmark dependency.
